842 entries, fazer-video to fdd-planning. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **fazer-video**: Transforma o vídeo gravado pelo aluno num Reel pronto para postar - corta pausas e erros, escreve a legenda, desenha as telas, monta, faz a prévia, escolhe a música com o aluno, confere tudo, passa no avaliador e entrega na pasta 3-videos-prontos. Use quando o aluno disser "faz meu vídeo", "gravei", "pus o vídeo na pasta", "edita meu vídeo", e para retomar um projeto parado entre o recebimento e a entrega. ([autonom-ia/video-edit-autonomia](https://github.com/autonom-ia/video-edit-autonomia/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/fazer-video) · 2 copies)
- **fazhi-deep-research**: 深度法律研究技能，基于六工具体系（法条检索、法规全文阅读、案例检索、 案例全文阅读、联网搜索、网页阅读）开展三源并行的系统性法律研究。 支持法条定位、类案检索、实务动态追踪、正反观点交叉验证，输出来源 可溯、论证严谨的法律回答。所有引用均自带可点击溯源链接。 ([ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills](https://github.com/ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills/tree/HEAD/workbuddy/archive/deleted/2026-09-16-180007/connectors/marketplace/connectors/fazhi-law/skills/fazhi-deep-research) · ★103 · 2 copies)
- **fazhi-law-mcp**: 法智 MCP 底层工具调用技能，统一封装 6 个法律数据 MCP 工具的调用方式： case_search（案例检索）、case_browser（案例全文阅读）、legal_article_search（法条综合检索）、 law_content_visit（法规全文阅读）、webpage_search（联网搜索）、webpage_visit（网页阅读）。 本技能不直接面向最终用户，仅供 case-search、law-search-neo、fazhi-deep-research 等业务技能调用。 ([ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills](https://github.com/ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills/tree/HEAD/workbuddy/archive/deleted/2026-09-16-180007/connectors/marketplace/connectors/fazhi-law/skills/fazhi-law-mcp) · ★103 · 2 copies)
- **fazhi-mcp**: 本技能是法智/快查共享 MCP 的内部数据工具调用层，统一封装 6 个法律数据工具及企业数据能力入口： case_search（案例检索）、case_browser（案例全文阅读）、legal_article_search（法条综合检索）、 law_content_visit（法规全文阅读）、webpage_search（联网搜索）、webpage_visit（网页阅读），以及 discover（企业能力发现）和 call（企业工具调用）。法智与快查是同一 Connector 和运行时 MCP 服务的两种业务称谓。 仅供 case-search、law-search、legal-research、legal-drafting、legal-opinion、legal-due-diligence 等上层业务技能在执行过程中按需调用。 本技能不直接面向最终用户，不要用它直接回答法律咨询、法条查询、案例检索、企业查询、深度研究、文书起草、尽职调查或案件可视化请求。 ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/tree/HEAD/connectors/fazhi-law/skills/fazhi-mcp) · ★455 · 3 copies)

- **fazxes**: Production scope gatekeeper v3 — catches secretly-MVP plans before they reach the audit pipeline. Covers: reading and replying to Messenger threads, bulk follow-up to prior-inquirer threads, posting listings to Marketplace, posting to Facebook Groups, bulk-commenting on group posts, joining groups, and searching for rental/room posts to comment on. FCR exhibits must show units, error (SED/LSD), and sample structure. Designs exhibits; it does not run the analysis. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Field-Crops-Research-Skills/skills/fcr-figures-and-tables) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **fcr-literature-positioning**: Use when positioning a Field Crops Research (FCR) manuscript against the agronomy and crop-science literature so it reads as a general contribution to field-crop science, not a local report. FCR requires new scientific insight of general relevance and rejects corroborative or merely descriptive work, so the literature must establish the gap and the agronomic stakes. Stakes the contribution; it does not write the lit review. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Field-Crops-Research-Skills/skills/fcr-literature-positioning) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **fcr-public-workflows**: Use Founder Control Room's user-scoped MCP tools to inspect only the connected user's permitted project evidence and prepare non-authorizing recommendations. ([jussray/founder-control-room](https://github.com/jussray/founder-control-room/tree/HEAD/plugins/fcr-public/skills/fcr-public-workflows) · ★1)
- **fcr-reporting-and-data-policy**: Use when preparing the reporting completeness and research-data materials for a Field Crops Research (FCR) manuscript. FCR requires a data-availability statement at submission, full agronomic reporting (cultivar, soil, weather vs. phenology, management, design), declaration of any generative-AI use, and supports data/methods co-submission to Data in Brief / MethodsX. Prepares the materials; it does not waive requirements. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Field-Crops-Research-Skills/skills/fcr-reporting-and-data-policy) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **fcr-review-process**: Use to understand how Field Crops Research (FCR) evaluates a manuscript — initial editor screening against the journal's scope boundary, the single-anonymized review model with typically two or more reviewers, and the common reasons for desk rejection (controlled-environment-only, single-site single-season, descriptive/local, out-of-scope species). Sets expectations and shapes the paper to survive review; it does not contact editors. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Field-Crops-Research-Skills/skills/fcr-review-process) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **fcr-revision-and-rebuttal**: Use when writing the response to a Field Crops Research (FCR) revision decision (major or minor) and revising the manuscript. FCR reviewers commonly press on scope/generality, statistics and G×E, reproducibility detail, and a discussion that interprets rather than repeats. The response must address every point while protecting the contribution. Structures the response letter; it does not fabricate new results. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Field-Crops-Research-Skills/skills/fcr-revision-and-rebuttal) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **fcr-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for Field Crops Research (FCR) via Editorial Manager — article-type selection, abstract (&lt;=400 words) and highlights (3-5 bullets of &lt;=85 characters), complete agronomic reporting, data-availability statement, generative-AI declaration, Elsevier formatting, and the cover letter. Final checks; it does not draft content. - **fda-510k-pathway**: FDA 510(k) 上市前通知路径规划与判断器 v2.2.1。输入器械描述，判断是否需要 510(k)、推荐提交类型、指导查找 predicate device、生成审核级 12 列实质等同对比表、列出完整提交文件清单。覆盖 2026 最新法规：FDA QMSR（2026-02-02 生效）、HFE 终版指南（2026-05-29 发布，2026-08-01 起强制三层分类）、PCCP（AI 器械，终版 2024-12-04）、Section 524B 网络安全（现行指南版 2026-02-03，QMSR 对齐）、eSTAR v7.0、FDA 认可共识标准清单 #66。新增审核级能力：实审退回原因库（12 项 Top 缺陷+对策）、性能测试标准映射、软件文档层级判定、提交前验收门。适用于准备进入美国市场的医疗器械制造商做上市路径判断与零整改提交时使用。触发词：FDA 510k、premarket notification、FDA注册、predicate device、实质等同、substantial equivalence、eSTAR、美国注册、PCCP、AI医疗器械、Section 524B、QMSR。 ([HalseyYang/Skills-HY](https://github.com/HalseyYang/Skills-HY/tree/HEAD/fda-510k-pathway) · ★1)
